+"use client";
+import Link from "next/link";
+import {useEffect,useRef,useState,type FormEvent} from "react";
+import type {ReleasePage,ReleaseRow} from "@/lib/releases/types";
+import styles from "./releases.module.css";
+const initial={sku:"",productId:"",batchId:"",state:"all"};
+const stateLabel=(value:string)=>value.replaceAll("_"," ");
+export function ReleaseWorkbenchClient() {
+  const [draft,setDraft]=useState(initial),[search,setSearch]=useState(initial),[cursor,setCursor]=useState(""),[page,setPage]=useState<ReleasePage|null>(null),[loading,setLoading]=useState(true),[error,setError]=useState(""),[csvAllowed,setCsvAllowed]=useState(false),[exporting,setExporting]=useState(false),[exportError,setExportError]=useState(""),[exportNotice,setExportNotice]=useState(""),[retry,setRetry]=useState(0);
+  const latest=useRef(0);
+  useEffect(()=>{const controller=new AbortController();fetch("/api/releases/status",{cache:"no-store",signal:controller.signal}).then(async response=>{if(response.ok){const value=await response.json();if(!controller.signal.aborted)setCsvAllowed(value.csvExportEnabled===true);}}).catch(()=>{});return()=>controller.abort();},[]);
+  useEffect(()=>{
+    const controller=new AbortController(),sequence=++latest.current,params=query(search,cursor);
+    fetch(`/api/releases?${params}`,{cache:"no-store",signal:controller.signal}).then(async response=>{const value=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw new Error(value.message??"The release workbench could not be loaded.");if(!controller.signal.aborted&&sequence===latest.current)setPage(value);}).catch(cause=>{if(!controller.signal.aborted&&sequence===latest.current)setError(cause instanceof Error?cause.message:"The release workbench could not be loaded.");}).finally(()=>{if(!controller.signal.aborted&&sequence===latest.current)setLoading(false);});
+    return()=>controller.abort();
+  },[search,cursor,retry]);
+  function beginSearch(){latest.current++;setLoading(true);setError("");setPage(null);setExportError("");setExportNotice("");}
+  function submit(event:FormEvent){event.preventDefault();beginSearch();setCursor("");setSearch({...draft});setRetry(n=>n+1);}
+  async function exportPage(){
+    setExporting(true);setExportError("");setExportNotice("");const sequence=latest.current;
+    try{const params=query(search,cursor);params.set("scope","page");const response=await fetch(`/api/releases/export?${params}`,{cache:"no-store"});if(!response.ok){const value=await response.json();throw new Error(value.message??"Export failed.");}const blob=await response.blob();if(sequence!==latest.current)return;const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement("a");a.href=url;a.download="pairvu-release-page.csv";a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);setExportNotice(response.headers.get("X-Release-Has-More")==="true"?"This bounded page was exported. More source records remain; continue the search to export later pages.":"This bounded page was exported; its cursor reached the end of the current search.");}
+    catch(cause){if(sequence===latest.current)setExportError(cause instanceof Error?cause.message:"Export failed.");}finally{setExporting(false);}
+  }
+  return <div className={styles.workbench}>
+    <header><p className="eyebrow">Release workbench</p><h1>Review release eligibility</h1><p>Compare the original machine result, current human decision and release rule. Eligibility does not publish your product.</p></header>
+    <form onSubmit={submit} className={styles.filters} aria-label="Release search">
+      <label>Frozen SKU<input name="sku" maxLength={80} value={draft.sku} onChange={e=>setDraft({...draft,sku:e.target.value})} placeholder="Exact SKU"/></label>
+      <label>Product ID<input name="productId" maxLength={128} value={draft.productId} onChange={e=>setDraft({...draft,productId:e.target.value})}/></label>
+      <label>Batch ID<input name="batchId" maxLength={128} value={draft.batchId} onChange={e=>setDraft({...draft,batchId:e.target.value})}/></label>
+      <label>Review state<select name="state" value={draft.state} onChange={e=>setDraft({...draft,state:e.target.value})}><option value="all">All records</option><option value="pending_review">Needs review</option><option value="blocked">Blocked by rule</option><option value="decided">Current approve or reject</option></select></label>
+      <button type="submit" disabled={loading}>Search</button>
+    </form>
+    {page?<section className={styles.policy} aria-label="Release rule"><strong>{page.policy.version}</strong><p>{page.policy.description}</p><details><summary>Rule and evaluation provenance</summary><p>Rule SHA-256: <code>{page.policy.hash}</code></p><p>Evaluated at {page.evaluatedAt}. Search pages use current decisions at request time.</p></details></section>:null}
+    {loading?<p role="status">Loading release records…</p>:error?<div role="alert"><p>{error}</p><button onClick={()=>{beginSearch();setRetry(n=>n+1);}}>Retry</button></div>:page?<>
+      <div className={styles.actions}><p>{page.rows.length} matching record{page.rows.length===1?"":"s"} in this page. Scanned {page.pagination.scanned} of at most {page.pagination.scanLimit} sources. Order: newest first, then analysis ID.</p><button onClick={exportPage} disabled={!csvAllowed||exporting}>{exporting?"Exporting…":"Export this search page (CSV)"}</button></div>
+      {!csvAllowed?<p>CSV export requires an active paid plan.</p>:<p>Export is limited to this search window (up to 20 matches). It re-evaluates current decisions; later pages require another export.</p>}
+      {exportError?<p role="alert">{exportError}</p>:null}{exportNotice?<p role="status">{exportNotice}</p>:null}
+      <div className={styles.rows}>{page.rows.map(row=><ReleaseCard key={row.analysisId} row={row}/>)}</div>
+      {!page.rows.length?<p role="status">{page.pagination.hasMore?"No matching records in this scan window. Continue searching remaining sources.":"No matching release records. Try another SKU, batch or review state."}</p>:null}
+      <div className={styles.pagination}>{page.pagination.hasMore?<><p>More sources remain. This page is not a complete search result.{page.pagination.scanWindowExhausted?" The scan boundary was reached.":""}</p><button onClick={()=>{beginSearch();setCursor(page.pagination.nextCursor!);}}>Continue search</button></>:<p>End of the current search.</p>}{cursor?<button onClick={()=>{beginSearch();setCursor("");}}>Back to first page</button>:null}</div>
+    </>:null}
+  </div>;
+}
+function query(search:typeof initial,cursor:string){const p=new URLSearchParams({state:search.state,limit:"20"});for(const key of ["sku","productId","batchId"] as const)if(search[key])p.set(key,search[key]);if(cursor)p.set("cursor",cursor);return p;}
+function ReleaseCard({row}:{row:ReleaseRow}) {
+  return <article className={styles.card} aria-label={`Release record ${row.analysisId}`}>
+    <div className={styles.source}><h2>{row.productSnapshot?.profile.name??"Analysis without a frozen product standard"}</h2><p>{row.productSnapshot?`Frozen SKU: ${row.productSnapshot.profile.skuLabel??"not recorded"} · standard v${row.productSnapshot.profileVersionNumber} · reference v${row.productSnapshot.referenceVersionNumber}`:"No frozen product identity is available."}</p><p>Analysis <code>{row.analysisId}</code>{row.batchId?<> · Batch <code>{row.batchId}</code></>:null}</p><Link href={`/?analysis=${encodeURIComponent(row.analysisId)}`}>View evidence and human review</Link></div>
+    <div className={styles.columns}>
+      <section><h3>Machine result</h3><strong>{row.machine.verdict?.toUpperCase()??"No verdict"}</strong><p>{stateLabel(row.machine.status)} · {row.machine.issueCount} product differences</p><p>Detection: {row.machine.qaEngineVersion??"unknown"}<br/>Risk: {row.machine.riskPolicyVersion??"unknown"}<br/>Model policy: {row.machine.modelPolicyVersion??"unknown"}</p>{row.machine.limitations.length?<ul>{row.machine.limitations.map((l,i)=><li key={i}>{l.type}: {l.message}</li>)}</ul>:null}<p>Reference image: {row.assets.referenceAvailable?"available":"unavailable"}<br/>Candidate image: {row.assets.candidateAvailable?"available":"unavailable"}</p></section>
+      <section><h3>Human decision</h3><strong>{row.human.action?stateLabel(row.human.action):"No current decision"}</strong><p>Revision {row.human.revision}</p>{row.human.reason?<p>{row.human.reason}</p>:null}{row.human.action==="revoke"?<p>The previous decision was revoked.</p>:null}</section>
+      <section><h3>Rule eligibility</h3><strong className={row.eligibility==="eligible"?styles.eligible:styles.blocked}>{stateLabel(row.eligibility)}</strong><ul>{row.reasons.map(reason=><li key={reason.code}>{reason.message}<small>{reason.code}</small></li>)}</ul><p>{row.policyVersion} · current evaluation</p></section>
+    </div>
+    <details><summary>Source and decision provenance</summary><p>Machine SHA-256: <code>{row.machine.sourceHash??"unavailable — bounded diagnostic source"}</code></p>{!row.machine.sourceComplete?<p>The full original source could not be read within supported limits and ownership boundaries. This record is blocked; its exported source is a bounded diagnostic snapshot.</p>:null}<p>Rule SHA-256: <code>{row.policyHash}</code></p><p>Reviewed machine snapshot: <code>{row.human.machineHash??"none"}</code></p><p>Evaluated at {row.evaluatedAt}; human revision {row.human.revision}.</p></details>
+  </article>;
+}

+import type {Metadata} from "next";
+import {headers} from "next/headers";
+import {redirect,notFound} from "next/navigation";
+import {getVisualQAEnv} from "@/lib/cloudflare/bindings";
+import {resolveRequestAccess,RequestAccessError} from "@/lib/auth/request-access";
+import {releasesEnabled,requireReleaseOwner} from "@/lib/releases/repository";
+import {ReleaseError} from "@/lib/releases/types";
+import {AccountWorkspaceNav} from "@/components/AccountWorkspaceNav";
+import {ReleaseWorkbenchClient} from "./ReleaseWorkbenchClient";
+export const dynamic="force-dynamic";
+export const metadata:Metadata={title:"Release workbench",robots:{index:false,follow:false}};
+export default async function ReleaseWorkbenchPage() {
+  const env=getVisualQAEnv();let access;
+  try {access=await resolveRequestAccess(env,await headers(),undefined);}catch(error){if(error instanceof RequestAccessError)redirect("/sign-in?next=/account/releases");throw error;}
+  if(!releasesEnabled(env))notFound();
+  try{await requireReleaseOwner(env.VISUALQA_DB,access);}catch(error){if(error instanceof ReleaseError)notFound();throw error;}
+  return <main className="account-page"><div className="account-shell"><AccountWorkspaceNav/><ReleaseWorkbenchClient/></div></main>;
+}

+import {NextResponse} from "next/server";
+import {getWorkspaceBatchEntitlement} from "@/lib/accounts/repository";
+import {releaseAccess,releaseFailure,releaseHeaders} from "@/lib/releases/http";
+import {listReleases,parseReleaseFilters} from "@/lib/releases/repository";
+import {releaseCsv} from "@/lib/releases/csv";
+import {ReleaseError} from "@/lib/releases/types";
+export const dynamic="force-dynamic";
+export async function GET(request:Request) {
+  try {
+    const {env,access}=await releaseAccess(request);const filters=parseReleaseFilters(new URL(request.url).searchParams,true);
+    let allowed=false;try{allowed=(await getWorkspaceBatchEntitlement(env.VISUALQA_DB,access.workspaceId!)).csvExportEnabled;}catch{/* Fail closed for invalid subscription. */}
+    if(!allowed)throw new ReleaseError("csv_export_upgrade_required","CSV export is included with active paid plans.",403);
+    const page=await listReleases(env.VISUALQA_DB,access,filters);
+    return new NextResponse(releaseCsv(page),{headers:{...releaseHeaders,"Content-Type":"text/csv; charset=utf-8","Content-Disposition":"attachment; filename=pairvu-release-page.csv","X-Release-Row-Count":String(page.rows.length),"X-Release-Has-More":String(page.pagination.hasMore),"X-Release-Next-Cursor":page.pagination.nextCursor??"","X-Release-Scan-Limit":String(page.pagination.scanLimit),"X-Release-Scanned":String(page.pagination.scanned),"X-Release-Policy-Hash":page.policy.hash}});
+  }catch(error){return releaseFailure(error);}
+}

+# UPG-06 release workbench contract
+
+Developer scope: local implementation and DEV_READY evidence; independent acceptance binds the candidate Git SHA. No production release or feature enablement is authorized. RELEASE_WORKBENCH_ENABLED is exact-string true and defaults false in production config. No schema migration is necessary: workspace analysis, child, snapshot and review-head indexes already support the reads.
+
+## Immutable rule and priority
+
+release-policy-001 is a built-in manifest with canonical JSON SHA-256. Its exact supported versions are engine m0-qa-engine-008, risk m0-risk-policy-003, model policy m0-model-policy-002, OpenAI gpt-4.1-mini, prompt m0-real-mvp-009+regions-003, identity guard identity-evidence-004 and regions schema 1. Version changes require a new manifest version and code review. Clients cannot supply policy/version/actor/workspace overrides.
+
+Priority: integrity/source/evidence failures, noncompleted analyses, unavailable images, explicit reject, high/critical issues => blocked. Otherwise absent approve (including revoke) => needs_review. Only complete supported evidence plus a current source-matching approve => eligible. All reasons remain visible, including limitations, original machine verdict and human revision. Eligibility is a current rule evaluation, never an external publication or historical mutation. PASS alone cannot release.
+
+Completeness requires nonempty unique known selected checks; exactly one observation per selected check and no extras; bounded source (24 rows per child type, 64 KiB combined evidence/model usage, 1 KiB selected-check JSON, 32 KiB product snapshot, 256 KiB reviewed snapshot); all storage budgets count UTF-8 bytes through SQLite length(CAST(text AS BLOB)), including embedded NUL, with the exact boundary accepted and boundary+1 rejected; match/mismatch with observable reference and candidate, sufficient coverage, recognized confidence/difference kind, nonempty explanation, both observations, comparison summary and visible evidence; supported identity guard and comparable identity evidence for logo/text; well-formed asset-bound region pairs (unverified numeric regions are never treated as localization proof); at least one successful supported model call bound to both input assets; no limitations. Recomputed risk verdict and issue identities/severity/confidence must match the stored machine result. Unknown, malformed, missing, uncertain, not-observable and not-applicable evidence all fail closed. This deliberately conservative first rule does not interpret not-applicable as complete evidence. The current risk policy assigns high/critical to all product differences, so such REVIEW records cannot become eligible. Future valid complete REVIEW records are not rejected solely for their verdict, but require a separately reviewed supported policy version.
+
+## Source, ownership and consistency
+
+Only server sessions resolved by existing auth/account logic identify workspace/user. Every data read additionally requires active workspace owner membership. Anonymous requests return 401; inactive/foreign owners receive 404. List/export never accept client identities. Feature-off routes return 404 and navigation remains hidden. GETs do not write analyses or audit decisions. Detail links open the existing single-analysis review screen.
+
+One SQL SELECT per bounded scan returns machine rows, ordered child records, review head/current event, asset metadata, frozen product snapshot and batch association from one SQLite read snapshot. No later per-row queries stitch revisions. Invalid head/event pairs or foreign child ownership fail closed. Current original machine data are mapped with the same read-only normalization as the review writer; their canonical semantic snapshot must equal the SHA-verified approved audit snapshot. A source changed after approval requires a new human decision. machineSourceHash additionally binds the original machine data, selected checks and input assets. If bounds or ownership prevent a complete materialization, sourceComplete=false and sourceHash=null; the blocked record explicitly carries a bounded diagnostic snapshot instead of claiming a full original hash. Actor IDs, request IDs, email and private notes are not exported. DB availability checks do not independently verify R2 object existence or image interpretation.
+
+SKU/name/profile/reference versions come only from the frozen analysis snapshot, with original JSON SHA verification, normalized profile and identity/version binding validation against the owned immutable profile/reference rows. Product lifecycle/name fields are not used in eligibility. Current rename/archive is never joined into that identity. Unbound records explicitly have no frozen standard. Invalid or legacy-bound sources fail closed. Deleted/failed/expired/missing assets are unavailable and block release. Retention is evaluated by SQLite server time, not a client timestamp.
+
+## Complete bounded traversal
+
+Exact SKU (1–80 characters), productId/batchId (bounded safe identifiers), state all/pending_review/blocked/decided, limit 1–50 and opaque cursor are the only accepted parameters; duplicates/unknowns are rejected. pending_review means needs_review; blocked means blocked; decided means current approve or reject (may overlap blocked). Order is created_at DESC, id DESC. SQL applies workspace/frozen identity/batch constraints, then scans at most 100 source rows. Rule-state filtering can yield an empty page with a continuation. Cursor advances from the last consumed source row, never an arbitrary truncated match set. It is tied to server workspace and filters. scanWindowExhausted/scanned/hasMore/nextCursor explicitly describe boundaries; no guessed total. Following every continuation covers all matching records present in that ordering, including late matches after empty windows. Separate pages are evaluated at request time; concurrent inserts or decision changes do not constitute a frozen whole-workspace search session.
+
+## Export
+
+The actual existing active/trialing csvExportEnabled entitlement is required; free plans are denied. Export additionally requires explicit scope=page to acknowledge its bounded scan. Limit remains 1–50 and scan 100; headers expose row count, has-more, cursor and scan boundary. The CSV includes machine original versions/status/verdict/source snapshot/hash, current action/category/reason/revision/review machine hash, rule version/hash/eligibility/reason codes/evaluatedAt, original frozen product JSON/hash/version fields and batch ID. It excludes actor/private notes. Pages containing sourceComplete=false are rejected with export_source_incomplete (409), rather than silently exporting partial original evidence. UI export is explicitly one bounded search page, not all workspace results; following cursors exports remaining pages. Concurrent review writes return wholly before or wholly after a committed decision, never a mixed row. Every CSV cell is quoted; formula-looking prefixes after Unicode normalization/leading whitespace/control or leading tab/newline receive a literal apostrophe. Quotes/newlines are escaped without interpreting user data. Existing batch CSV uses the same safety helper.
+
+## Validation limits
+
+Evidence uses synthetic owned users/assets/model records in actual local D1 with all production migrations and actual routes/repositories, and actual React components in desktop/narrow browsers. No remote DB, real auth session, R2, model, payment or external publication is tested or invoked. Independent fixed-SHA QA remains required. Production, deployment and enablement remain separate future gates.

+++ b/fixtures/release-workbench/sql-worker.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,6 @@
+// Local synthetic D1 transport; never opens cloud resources.
+export default {async fetch(request,env) {
+  if(new URL(request.url).pathname==='/b'&&env.PEER)return env.PEER.fetch(request);
+  try {const {statements}=await request.json();const results=await env.DB.batch(statements.map(s=>env.DB.prepare(s.sql).bind(...s.params)));return Response.json({success:true,worker:env.NAME,results});}
+  catch(error){return Response.json({success:false,worker:env.NAME,error:error.message});}
+}};

+  await test("paid export explicitly uses current applied bounded page and scope",()=>{const q=requests.filter(r=>r.path==="/api/releases/export").at(-1)!.query as Record<string,string>;check(q.scope==="page"&&q.sku==="=FROZEN-SKU"&&q.limit==="20","Export mismatch");check(downloads.length===1,"Download missing");check(root.textContent?.includes("More source records remain"),"Export continuation hidden");});
+  failExport=true;await click(button("Export this"));await test("export permission failure appears visibly",()=>check(root.querySelector('[role="alert"]')?.textContent?.includes("active paid plans"),"Export error hidden"));failExport=false;
+  scenario="continuation";await search();await test("empty scan is not presented as complete search",()=>{check(root.textContent?.includes("No matching records in this scan window"),"Empty continuation hidden");check(button("Continue search"),"Missing continuation");check(root.textContent?.includes("not a complete search result"),"Truncation silent");});
+  await click(button("Continue search"));await test("continuation finds later rows and reaches explicit end",()=>{check(root.querySelectorAll("article").length===sample.rows.length,"Late rows skipped");check(root.textContent?.includes("End of the current search"),"End missing");check((requests.filter(r=>r.path==="/api/releases").at(-1)!.query as Record<string,string>).cursor==="synthetic-next","Cursor missing");});
+  scenario="empty";await click(button("Back to first"));await test("terminal empty state suggests changed filters",()=>check(root.textContent?.includes("No matching release records. Try another SKU"),"Empty state missing"));
+  scenario="error";await search();await test("D1 error has retry without stale eligible rows",()=>{check(root.querySelector('[role="alert"]')?.textContent?.includes("Synthetic D1 read unavailable"),"Error hidden");check(root.querySelectorAll("article").length===0,"Stale rows retained");});
+  scenario="normal";await click(button("Retry"));await test("retry recovers actual component",()=>check(root.querySelectorAll("article").length===1,"Retry failed"));
+  paid=false;await mount();await test("free plan disables CSV but retains readable workbench",()=>{check(button("Export this").disabled,"Free export enabled");check(root.textContent?.includes("requires an active paid plan"),"Paid explanation missing");check(root.querySelectorAll("article").length===1,"Free read hidden");});
+  enabled=false;await mount();await test("feature off hides navigation and rejects workbench",()=>{check(!root.querySelector('a[href="/account/releases"]'),"Disabled nav still shown");check(root.textContent?.includes("currently unavailable"),"Disabled route not visible");});
+  enabled=true;paid=true;scenario="normal";await mount();
+  await test("desktop/narrow layout has no horizontal overflow or clipped cards",()=>{check(document.documentElement.scrollWidth<=window.innerWidth+1,"Horizontal viewport overflow");for(const card of root.querySelectorAll("article")){const rect=card.getBoundingClientRect();check(rect.left>=0&&rect.right<=window.innerWidth+1,"Card clipped");}const cols=root.querySelector('h3')!.parentElement!.parentElement!,layout=getComputedStyle(cols).gridTemplateColumns;if(mode==="narrow")check(layout.split(" ").length===1,"Narrow layout not stacked");else check(layout.split(" ").length===3,"Desktop columns not separate");});
+  await test("no unexpected browser errors or machine/review writes",()=>{check(errors.length===0,errors.join("\n"));check(requests.every(r=>r.method==="GET"),"Workbench wrote a decision");});
+ }catch(error){status="FAIL";failure=String(error);}
+ const report={unitId:"UPG-06",synthetic:true,status,mode,viewport:{width:innerWidth,height:innerHeight},cases,requests,errors,failure,limitations:["Actual React workbench and navigation with controlled API responses and Next Link/navigation boundaries.","Route/D1 ownership, rule and concurrency proof recorded separately; no production authentication, model, R2 or payment."]};

+const server=createServer(async(req,res)=>{
+ const url=new URL(req.url,'http://localhost');
+ if(url.pathname==='/app.js'){res.setHeader('Content-Type','text/javascript');res.end(bundled.outputFiles.find(f=>f.path.endsWith('.js')).text);return;}
+ if(url.pathname==='/local.css'){res.setHeader('Content-Type','text/css');res.end(bundled.outputFiles.find(f=>f.path.endsWith('.css')).text);return;}
+ if(url.pathname==='/style.css'){res.setHeader('Content-Type','text/css');res.end(await readFile('app/globals.css'));return;}
+ if(url.pathname==='/page.json'){res.setHeader('Content-Type','application/json');res.end(await readFile('fixtures/release-workbench/sample-page.json'));return;}
+ if(url.pathname==='/__results'&&req.method==='POST'){let body='';for await(const chunk of req)body+=chunk;const report={...JSON.parse(body),sources};await writeFile(resolve(`fixtures/release-workbench/browser-${report.mode}-results.json`),JSON.stringify(report,null,2)+'\n');res.end('recorded');console.log(`${report.status}: ${report.cases.length} ${report.mode} cases`);return;}

+});
+server.listen(Number(process.argv[2]??0),'127.0.0.1',()=>console.log(`Release workbench fixture: http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`));

+import {ReleaseError,type ReleasePage} from "./types";
+/** Excel/Sheets formula prefixes may follow invisible whitespace or Unicode forms. */
+export function csvCell(value:unknown):string {
+  const raw=String(value??""),probe=raw.normalize("NFKC").replace(/^[\s\p{Cf}\p{Cc}]+/u,"");
+  const safe=/^[=+\-@]/u.test(probe)||/^[\t\r\n]/u.test(raw)?`'${raw}`:raw;
+  return `"${safe.replaceAll('"','""')}"`;
+}
+export function releaseCsv(page:ReleasePage) {
+  if(page.rows.some(row=>!row.machine.sourceComplete))throw new ReleaseError("export_source_incomplete","A full original source could not be read safely for this page. Refine the search or review the blocked records; a partial original snapshot cannot be exported.",409);
+  const header=["analysis_id","batch_id","created_at","machine_status","machine_verdict","qa_engine_version","risk_policy_version","model_policy_version","machine_source_hash","machine_source_complete","machine_source_snapshot_json","human_action","human_category","human_reason","human_revision","human_decided_at","human_machine_snapshot_hash","release_policy_version","release_policy_hash","eligibility","reason_codes","reason_messages","evaluated_at","reference_available","candidate_available","product_id","frozen_sku","frozen_product_name","profile_version_id","profile_version_number","reference_version_id","reference_version_number","product_bound_at","product_snapshot_hash","product_snapshot_json"];
+  const rows=page.rows.map(r=>[r.analysisId,r.batchId,r.createdAt,r.machine.status,r.machine.verdict,r.machine.qaEngineVersion,r.machine.riskPolicyVersion,r.machine.modelPolicyVersion,r.machine.sourceHash,r.machine.sourceComplete,r.machine.sourceSnapshotJson,r.human.action,r.human.category,r.human.reason,r.human.revision,r.human.createdAt,r.human.machineHash,r.policyVersion,r.policyHash,r.eligibility,r.reasons.map(v=>v.code).join("; "),r.reasons.map(v=>v.message).join("; "),r.evaluatedAt,r.assets.referenceAvailable,r.assets.candidateAvailable,r.productSnapshot?.productId,r.productSnapshot?.profile.skuLabel,r.productSnapshot?.profile.name,r.productSnapshot?.profileVersionId,r.productSnapshot?.profileVersionNumber,r.productSnapshot?.referenceVersionId,r.productSnapshot?.referenceVersionNumber,r.productSnapshot?.boundAt,r.productSnapshotHash,r.productSnapshotJson]);
+  return [header,...rows].map(row=>row.map(csvCell).join(",")).join("\r\n");
+}

+import {NextResponse} from "next/server";
+import {resolveRequestAccess,RequestAccessError} from "@/lib/auth/request-access";
+import {getVisualQAEnv} from "@/lib/cloudflare/bindings";
+import {ReleaseError} from "./types";
+import {releasesEnabled,requireReleaseOwner} from "./repository";
+export const releaseHeaders={"Cache-Control":"private, no-store","Vary":"Cookie"};
+export async function releaseAccess(request:Request) {
+  const env=getVisualQAEnv(),access=await resolveRequestAccess(env,request.headers,undefined);
+  if(!releasesEnabled(env))throw new ReleaseError("release_workbench_disabled","Release workbench is currently unavailable.",404);
+  await requireReleaseOwner(env.VISUALQA_DB,access);return {env,access};
+}
+export function releaseFailure(error:unknown) {
+  if(error instanceof RequestAccessError)return NextResponse.json({error:"authentication_required",message:"Sign in to use the release workbench."},{status:401,headers:releaseHeaders});
+  if(error instanceof ReleaseError)return NextResponse.json({error:error.code,message:error.message},{status:error.status,headers:releaseHeaders});
+  console.error("release_workbench_read_failed",error);
+  return NextResponse.json({error:"release_workbench_unavailable",message:"The release workbench could not be loaded. Try again."},{status:503,headers:releaseHeaders});
+}

+import {mapStoredObservation,mapStoredIssue,mapStoredLimitation,mapStoredModelCall} from "@/lib/analysis/repository";
+import {normalizeBoundEvidence,normalizeRegionPair} from "@/lib/qa/evidence-regions";
+import {identityEvidenceProblem} from "@/lib/qa/identity-evidence";
+import {M0RiskPolicy} from "@/lib/qa/m0-policy";
+import {normalizeProductProfile} from "@/lib/products/profile-validation";
+import type {M0CheckFamily} from "@/lib/qa/types";
+import type {ProductSnapshot} from "@/lib/products/types";
+import type {ReleaseRow} from "./types";

+// Changing any accepted version or priority requires a new built-in rule version.
+const MANIFEST_JSON='{"version":"release-policy-001","engine":"m0-qa-engine-008","risk":"m0-risk-policy-003","modelPolicy":"m0-model-policy-002","provider":"openai","model":"gpt-4.1-mini","prompt":"m0-real-mvp-009+regions-003","identityGuard":"identity-evidence-004","regions":1,"priority":["integrity_or_incomplete","reject","high_or_critical","missing_current_approve","eligible"],"completeness":"unique selected checks; fully observable match/mismatch; supported identity and asset-bound region schemas; matching risk derivation; supported successful model calls; no limitations; available images; frozen product integrity; source-matching review"}';
+const manifest=JSON.parse(MANIFEST_JSON) as Record<string,string>;
+export async function sha256(text:string){const bytes=await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256",new TextEncoder().encode(text));return Array.from(new Uint8Array(bytes),n=>n.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join("");}
+export function canonical(value:unknown):string {
+  if(Array.isArray(value))return `[${value.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
+  if(value&&typeof value==="object")return `{${Object.entries(value).filter(([,v])=>v!==undefined).sort(([a],[b])=>a<b?-1:a>b?1:0).map(([k,v])=>`${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(v)}`).join(",")}}`;
+  return JSON.stringify(value)??"null";
+}
+export async function releasePolicy(){return {version:manifest.version,hash:await sha256(canonical(JSON.parse(MANIFEST_JSON))),description:"Complete supported machine evidence, available images and a current source-matching human approval are required. High/critical differences and limitations block eligibility. Eligibility does not publish externally."};}
+export type StoredReleaseRow=Record<string,unknown>;
+function parsed(value:unknown):unknown {try{return JSON.parse(String(value));}catch{return null;}}
+function records(value:unknown):Record<string,unknown>[] {const p=parsed(value);return Array.isArray(p)?p.filter(v=>!!v&&typeof v==="object"&&!Array.isArray(v)):[];}
+function text(value:unknown):value is string{return typeof value==="string"&&value.trim().length>0;}
+function date(value:unknown){return text(value)&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value));}
+const checks=["logo","visible_text","quantity","dominant_color","major_components","major_shape_packaging"];
+export async function evaluateRelease(row:StoredReleaseRow,evaluatedAt:string):Promise<ReleaseRow> {
+  const policy=await releasePolicy(),reasons:ReleaseRow["reasons"]=[];
+  const add=(code:string,message:string)=>{if(!reasons.some(r=>r.code===code))reasons.push({code,message});};
+  const sourceComplete=Number(row.overflow)===0&&Number(row.foreignChildren)===0;
+  const raw={sourceComplete,analysisId:row.id,status:row.status,verdict:row.verdict,qaEngineVersion:row.qaEngineVersion,riskPolicyVersion:row.riskPolicyVersion,modelPolicyVersion:row.modelPolicyVersion,completedAt:row.completedAt,selectedChecks:parsed(row.selectedChecksJson),referenceAssetId:row.referenceAssetId,candidateAssetId:row.candidateAssetId,observations:records(row.observations),productIssues:records(row.issues),limitations:records(row.limitations),modelCalls:records(row.modelCalls)};
+  const assets={referenceAssetId:String(row.referenceAssetId),candidateAssetId:String(row.candidateAssetId)};
+  const observations=raw.observations.map(mapStoredObservation),issues=raw.productIssues.map(mapStoredIssue),limitations=raw.limitations.map(mapStoredLimitation),calls=raw.modelCalls.map(mapStoredModelCall);
+  const currentSnapshot={analysisId:String(row.id),verdict:row.verdict,qaEngineVersion:row.qaEngineVersion,riskPolicyVersion:row.riskPolicyVersion,modelPolicyVersion:row.modelPolicyVersion,completedAt:row.completedAt,observations:observations.map(o=>({...o,evidence:normalizeBoundEvidence(o.evidence,assets,{reference:o.observability.reference==="not_observable"?false:o.evidence.referenceVisible,candidate:o.observability.candidate==="not_observable"?false:o.evidence.candidateVisible})})),productIssues:issues.map(i=>({...i,evidence:normalizeBoundEvidence(i.evidence,assets,{reference:i.sourceObservability.reference==="not_observable"?false:i.evidence.referenceVisible,candidate:i.sourceObservability.candidate==="not_observable"?false:i.evidence.candidateVisible})})),limitations:limitations.map(l=>({...l,evidence:normalizeBoundEvidence(l.evidence,assets,{reference:l.evidence.referenceVisible,candidate:l.evidence.candidateVisible})})),modelCalls:calls};
+  if(row.status!=="completed")add("analysis_not_completed","Analysis is not completed; queued, running and failed results cannot release.");
+  if(!date(row.completedAt)||!["pass","review","fail"].includes(String(row.verdict)))add("machine_summary_invalid","Machine verdict or completion timestamp is missing or invalid.");
+  if(row.qaEngineVersion!==manifest.engine||row.riskPolicyVersion!==manifest.risk||row.modelPolicyVersion!==manifest.modelPolicy)add("machine_version_unsupported","Original detection versions are missing or unsupported by this release rule.");
+  if(Number(row.foreignChildren)>0)add("source_ownership_invalid","Analysis evidence has an inconsistent ownership binding.");
+  if(Number(row.overflow)>0)add("machine_source_over_limit","Machine source exceeds the supported bounded evidence size.");
+  const selected=Array.isArray(raw.selectedChecks)?raw.selectedChecks:[];
+  if(!selected.length||selected.length>6||new Set(selected).size!==selected.length||selected.some(c=>typeof c!=="string"||!checks.includes(c))||observations.length!==selected.length||selected.some(c=>observations.filter(o=>o.checkType===c).length!==1))add("selected_checks_incomplete","Every unique selected check needs exactly one supported observation.");
+  for(const o of observations) {
+    if(!["match","mismatch"].includes(o.status)||o.observability.reference!=="observable"||o.observability.candidate!=="observable"||o.observability.coverage!=="sufficient")add("observation_insufficient","Uncertain, not-applicable or insufficiently observable evidence cannot support eligibility.");
+    if(!["high","medium","low"].includes(o.confidence)||!text(o.explanation)||!text(o.evidence.referenceObservation)||!text(o.evidence.candidateObservation)||!text(o.evidence.comparisonSummary)||!Array.isArray(o.evidence.visibleEvidence)||!o.evidence.visibleEvidence.length||o.evidence.visibleEvidence.some(v=>!text(v))||(o.status==="match"&&o.differenceKind!=="none")||!o.differenceKind)add("observation_invalid","Observation structure, explanation or comparison evidence is incomplete.");
+    if(o.evidence.referenceVisible===false||o.evidence.candidateVisible===false)add("observation_insufficient","Evidence reports an unavailable view of the requested attribute.");
+    if(["logo","visible_text"].includes(o.checkType)) {
+      let valid=false;try{valid=o.evidence.identityEvidence?.validationVersion===manifest.identityGuard&&identityEvidenceProblem(o)===null;}catch{/* Malformed historical evidence is blocked. */}
+      if(!valid)add("identity_evidence_invalid","Logo/text identity evidence or its application guard version is unsupported.");
+    }
+    const pair=normalizeRegionPair(o.evidence.regions,assets);
+    if(!o.evidence.regions||pair.reference.reason==="invalid_region"||pair.candidate.reason==="invalid_region")add("region_schema_invalid","Evidence regions must use the supported schema and original input asset identities.");
+  }
+  if(!calls.length||calls.some(c=>c.status!=="completed"||c.provider!==manifest.provider||c.model!==manifest.model||c.promptVersion!==manifest.prompt||c.modelPolicyVersion!==manifest.modelPolicy||c.purpose!=="analysis"||c.inputAssetIds.length!==2||!c.inputAssetIds.includes(assets.referenceAssetId)||!c.inputAssetIds.includes(assets.candidateAssetId)||c.errorCode||c.errorMessage))add("model_source_unsupported","Successful model provenance bound to both original images is missing or unsupported.");
+  if(limitations.length)add("machine_limitations_present","Machine limitations remain unresolved and block this conservative release rule.");
+  // Independently verify persisted product issue severity against the original risk policy.
+  try {
+    const derived=new M0RiskPolicy().decide({analysisId:String(row.id),selectedChecks:selected as M0CheckFamily[],observations,limitations:[]});
+    const tuple=(i:{type:string;sourceCheckType:string;severity:string;confidence:string;sourceDifferenceKind?:string})=>[i.type,i.sourceCheckType,i.sourceDifferenceKind,i.severity,i.confidence];
+    if(derived.limitations.length>0||derived.verdict!==row.verdict||canonical(derived.productIssues.map(tuple))!==canonical(issues.map(tuple))||issues.some(i=>i.kind!=="product"||!text(i.message)||!observations.some(o=>(i.observationId===null||o.id===i.observationId)&&o.checkType===i.sourceCheckType&&canonical(o.observability)===canonical(i.sourceObservability)&&canonical(o.evidence)===canonical(i.evidence))))add("machine_derivation_invalid","Stored verdict or product differences do not match the supported risk derivation.");
+  } catch {add("machine_derivation_invalid","Stored machine evidence cannot be validated safely.");}
+  if(issues.some(i=>["high","critical"].includes(i.severity)))add("high_risk_difference","High or critical machine differences remain blocked even after human approval.");
+  const referenceAvailable=row.referenceAvailable===1,candidateAvailable=row.candidateAvailable===1;
+  if(!referenceAvailable||!candidateAvailable)add("assets_unavailable","Original reference or candidate image is deleted, failed, expired or unavailable.");
+  let productSnapshot:ProductSnapshot|null=null;
+  if(row.snapshotJson!==null) {
+    try {
+      const p=parsed(row.snapshotJson) as ProductSnapshot;
+      if(!p||p.schemaVersion!==1||p.productId!==row.productId||p.profileVersionId!==row.profileVersionId||p.referenceVersionId!==row.referenceVersionId||p.revision!==row.productRevision||p.boundAt!==row.boundAt||p.referenceAssetId!==assets.referenceAssetId||![p.revision,p.profileVersionNumber,p.referenceVersionNumber].every(n=>Number.isSafeInteger(n)&&n>0)||!["front","back","left_side","right_side","detail","other"].includes(p.referenceRole)||canonical(normalizeProductProfile(p.profile))!==canonical(p.profile)||await sha256(String(row.snapshotJson))!==row.snapshotHash)throw new Error("Invalid frozen snapshot");
+      productSnapshot=p;
+      if(row.snapshotBindingsValid!==1)add("product_snapshot_binding_invalid","Frozen profile/reference versions are missing or do not belong to this workspace and product.");
+    } catch {add("product_snapshot_invalid","Frozen product standard integrity or version binding is invalid.");}
+  } else if(row.legacyBound===1)add("product_snapshot_missing","This bound product/batch has no immutable product standard snapshot.");
+  if(Number(row.batchCount)>1||Number(row.foreignBindings)>0||(row.sourceBatchId&&row.sourceBatchId!==row.batchId))add("batch_binding_invalid","Batch source is inconsistent or ambiguous.");
+  const revision=Number(row.reviewRevision??0),action=row.action as string|null;
+  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(revision)||revision<0||(revision>0&&(Number(row.eventRevision)!==revision||!text(row.reviewEventId)||!["approve","reject","revoke"].includes(String(action))||!text(row.reason)||!date(row.reviewCreatedAt)))||(revision===0&&row.reviewEventId)||Number(row.reviewEventCount)!==revision)add("review_source_invalid","Current review head and audit event are inconsistent.");
+  if(revision>0) {
+    const approved=parsed(row.reviewSnapshot);
+    if(!approved||await sha256(String(row.reviewSnapshot))!==row.reviewMachineHash)add("review_snapshot_invalid","Current human decision has invalid original machine snapshot integrity.");
+    else if(canonical(approved)!==canonical(currentSnapshot))add("review_source_changed","Machine source changed after this decision; review the current source again.");
+  }
+  if(action==="reject")add("human_rejected","Current human decision rejects this analysis.");
+  const blocked=reasons.length>0;
+  if(action!=="approve")add("human_approval_required",action==="revoke"?"Approval was revoked; a new explicit decision is required.":"No current human approval exists; machine PASS alone cannot release.");
+  if(!blocked&&action==="approve")add("complete_source_approved","Complete supported evidence and a current source-matching human approval satisfy this rule.");
+  return {analysisId:String(row.id),createdAt:String(row.createdAt),batchId:row.batchId as string|null,productSnapshot,productSnapshotJson:row.snapshotJson as string|null,productSnapshotHash:row.snapshotHash as string|null,machine:{status:String(row.status),verdict:row.verdict as string|null,qaEngineVersion:row.qaEngineVersion as string|null,riskPolicyVersion:row.riskPolicyVersion as string|null,modelPolicyVersion:row.modelPolicyVersion as string|null,completedAt:row.completedAt as string|null,issueCount:issues.length,limitations:limitations.map(l=>({type:l.type,message:l.message})),sourceHash:sourceComplete?await sha256(canonical(raw)):null,sourceComplete,sourceSnapshotJson:JSON.stringify(raw)},assets:{referenceAvailable,candidateAvailable},human:{action:action??null,category:row.reviewCategory as string|null,reason:row.reason as string|null,revision,createdAt:row.reviewCreatedAt as string|null,machineHash:row.reviewMachineHash as string|null},eligibility:blocked?"blocked":action==="approve"?"eligible":"needs_review",reasons,evaluatedAt,policyVersion:policy.version,policyHash:policy.hash};

+import type {D1Database} from "@/lib/cloudflare/bindings";
+import type {RequestAccessContext} from "@/lib/auth/request-access";
+import {evaluateRelease,releasePolicy,sha256,canonical,type StoredReleaseRow} from "./policy";
+import {ReleaseError,type ReleaseFilters,type ReleasePage,type ReleaseRow} from "./types";
+
+export const RELEASE_SCAN_LIMIT=100;
+const safeId=/^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9_-]{0,127}$/;
+export function parseReleaseFilters(params:URLSearchParams,exporting=false):ReleaseFilters {
+  const allowed=["sku","productId","batchId","state","limit","cursor",...(exporting?["scope"]:[])];
+  for(const key of params.keys())if(!allowed.includes(key)||params.getAll(key).length!==1)throw new ReleaseError("invalid_filter","Unsupported or repeated search parameter.");
+  if(exporting&&params.get("scope")!=="page")throw new ReleaseError("export_scope_required","Export requires scope=page to acknowledge its bounded search window.");
+  const sku=params.get("sku")??"",productId=params.get("productId")??"",batchId=params.get("batchId")??"",state=params.get("state")??"all",limitText=params.get("limit")??"20",cursor=params.get("cursor")??"";
+  if(sku.length>80||/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f-\u009f]/u.test(sku)||sku&&sku.trim()!==sku||[productId,batchId].some(id=>id!==""&&!safeId.test(id))||!["all","pending_review","blocked","decided"].includes(state)||!/^([1-9]|[1-4][0-9]|50)$/.test(limitText)||cursor.length>2048)throw new ReleaseError("invalid_filter","Use exact SKU, bounded identifiers, a supported state and limit 1–50.");
+  return {sku,productId,batchId,state:state as ReleaseFilters["state"],limit:Number(limitText),cursor};
+}
+export function releasesEnabled(env:{RELEASE_WORKBENCH_ENABLED?:string}){return env.RELEASE_WORKBENCH_ENABLED==="true";}
+export async function requireReleaseOwner(db:D1Database,access:RequestAccessContext) {
+  if(!access.authenticated||!access.workspaceId||!access.userId)throw new ReleaseError("authentication_required","Sign in to use the release workbench.",401);
+  const owner=await db.prepare("SELECT w.id FROM workspaces w JOIN workspace_memberships m ON m.workspace_id=w.id WHERE w.id=? AND w.status='active' AND m.user_id=? AND m.role='owner'").bind(access.workspaceId,access.userId).first();
+  if(!owner)throw new ReleaseError("workspace_not_found","Workspace not found.",404);
+  return {workspaceId:access.workspaceId,userId:access.userId};
+}
+const observationFields={id:"id",checkType:"check_type",status:"status",differenceKind:"difference_kind",referenceObservability:"reference_observability",candidateObservability:"candidate_observability",coverage:"coverage",confidence:"confidence",evidenceJson:"evidence_json",explanation:"explanation"};
+const issueFields={id:"id",observationId:"observation_id",kind:"issue_kind",type:"issue_type",sourceCheckType:"source_check_type",sourceDifferenceKind:"source_difference_kind",sourceReferenceObservability:"source_reference_observability",sourceCandidateObservability:"source_candidate_observability",sourceCoverage:"source_coverage",severity:"severity",confidence:"confidence",message:"message",evidenceJson:"evidence_json"};
+const limitationFields={id:"id",observationId:"observation_id",type:"limitation_type",sourceCheckType:"source_check_type",confidence:"confidence",message:"message",evidenceJson:"evidence_json"};
+const modelFields={id:"id",provider:"provider",purpose:"purpose",model:"model",promptVersion:"prompt_version",modelPolicyVersion:"model_policy_version",status:"status",errorCode:"error_code",errorMessage:"error_message",inputAssetIdsJson:"input_asset_ids_json",inputUsageJson:"input_usage_json",outputUsageJson:"output_usage_json",latencyMs:"latency_ms",estimatedCostUsd:"estimated_cost_usd"};
+const children=["analysis_observations","analysis_issues","analysis_limitations","analysis_model_calls"];
+// Exactly one SELECT binds each row's machine, human revision and frozen sources.
+function aggregate(table:string,fields:Record<string,string>){return `(SELECT json_group_array(json_object(${Object.entries(fields).map(([key,col])=>`'${key}',c.${col}`).join(",")})) FROM (SELECT * FROM ${table} WHERE analysis_id=a.id AND workspace_id=a.workspace_id ORDER BY created_at ASC,rowid ASC LIMIT 24) c)`;}
+// SQLite length(TEXT) counts characters (and stops at NUL); BLOB length counts
+// the actual UTF-8 bytes. Use the same expression in every budget and projection.
+const utf8Bytes=(column:string)=>`length(CAST(${column} AS BLOB))`;
+const evidenceBytes=["analysis_observations","analysis_issues","analysis_limitations"].map(table=>`COALESCE((SELECT SUM(${utf8Bytes("evidence_json")}) FROM ${table} WHERE analysis_id=a.id),0)`).join("+");
+const modelBytes=`COALESCE((SELECT SUM(${["input_usage_json","output_usage_json","input_asset_ids_json"].map(column=>`COALESCE(${utf8Bytes(column)},0)`).join("+")}) FROM analysis_model_calls WHERE analysis_id=a.id),0)`;
+const tooLarge=children.map(t=>`(SELECT COUNT(*)>24 FROM ${t} WHERE analysis_id=a.id)`).join(" OR ")+` OR ${evidenceBytes}+${modelBytes}>65536`;
+const selectedChecksBytes=utf8Bytes("a.selected_checks_json"),productSnapshotBytes=utf8Bytes("s.snapshot_json"),reviewSnapshotBytes=utf8Bytes("e.machine_snapshot_json");
+const bounded=(table:string,fields:Record<string,string>)=>`CASE WHEN ${tooLarge} THEN '[]' ELSE ${aggregate(table,fields)} END`;
+const foreignChildren=children.map(t=>`(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ${t} WHERE analysis_id=a.id AND workspace_id IS NOT a.workspace_id)`).join("+");
+const available=(alias:string,kind:string)=>`CASE WHEN ${alias}.id IS NOT NULL AND ${alias}.kind='${kind}' AND ${alias}.asset_type='image' AND ${alias}.status IN ('uploaded','normalized','analyzed') AND ${alias}.deleted_at IS NULL AND (${alias}.retention_expires_at IS NULL OR julianday(${alias}.retention_expires_at)>julianday('now')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END`;
+const projection=`SELECT a.id,a.created_at AS createdAt,a.status,a.verdict,a.reference_asset_id AS referenceAssetId,a.candidate_asset_id AS candidateAssetId,CASE WHEN ${selectedChecksBytes}<=1024 THEN a.selected_checks_json ELSE NULL END AS selectedChecksJson,a.qa_engine_version AS qaEngineVersion,a.risk_policy_version AS riskPolicyVersion,a.model_policy_version AS modelPolicyVersion,a.completed_at AS completedAt,
+ ${bounded("analysis_observations",observationFields)} AS observations,${bounded("analysis_issues",issueFields)} AS issues,${bounded("analysis_limitations",limitationFields)} AS limitations,${bounded("analysis_model_calls",modelFields)} AS modelCalls,(${foreignChildren}) AS foreignChildren,CASE WHEN ${tooLarge} OR ${selectedChecksBytes}>1024 OR ${productSnapshotBytes}>32768 OR ${reviewSnapshotBytes}>262144 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS overflow,
+ ${available("ra","reference")} AS referenceAvailable,${available("ca","candidate")} AS candidateAvailable,
+ CASE WHEN ${productSnapshotBytes}<=32768 THEN s.snapshot_json ELSE NULL END AS snapshotJson,s.snapshot_sha256 AS snapshotHash,s.product_id AS productId,s.product_revision AS productRevision,s.profile_version_id AS profileVersionId,s.reference_version_id AS referenceVersionId,s.bound_at AS boundAt,s.source_batch_id AS sourceBatchId,
+ CASE WHEN s.analysis_id IS NULL THEN 1 WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM product_profile_versions pv JOIN product_reference_versions rv ON rv.product_id=pv.product_id AND rv.workspace_id=pv.workspace_id JOIN products p ON p.id=pv.product_id AND p.workspace_id=pv.workspace_id WHERE pv.workspace_id=a.workspace_id AND pv.product_id=s.product_id AND pv.id=s.profile_version_id AND rv.id=s.reference_version_id AND rv.asset_id=a.reference_asset_id AND pv.schema_version=1 AND pv.source='user_confirmed' AND CASE WHEN json_valid(s.snapshot_json) THEN pv.version_number=json_extract(s.snapshot_json,'$.profileVersionNumber') AND rv.version_number=json_extract(s.snapshot_json,'$.referenceVersionNumber') AND rv.reference_role=json_extract(s.snapshot_json,'$.referenceRole') AND json(pv.profile_json)=json_extract(s.snapshot_json,'$.profile') ELSE 0 END) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS snapshotBindingsValid,
+ (SELECT MIN(bi.batch_id) FROM batch_items bi JOIN batches b ON b.id=bi.batch_id AND b.workspace_id=bi.workspace_id WHERE bi.analysis_id=a.id AND bi.workspace_id=a.workspace_id) AS batchId,
+ (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM batch_items bi JOIN batches b ON b.id=bi.batch_id AND b.workspace_id=bi.workspace_id WHERE bi.analysis_id=a.id AND bi.workspace_id=a.workspace_id) AS batchCount,
+ CASE WHEN EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM batch_items bi JOIN batches b ON b.id=bi.batch_id AND b.workspace_id=bi.workspace_id WHERE bi.analysis_id=a.id AND bi.workspace_id=a.workspace_id AND b.product_id IS NOT NULL) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS legacyBound,
+ ((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_product_snapshots WHERE analysis_id=a.id AND workspace_id IS NOT a.workspace_id)+(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM review_heads WHERE analysis_id=a.id AND workspace_id IS NOT a.workspace_id)+(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM review_events WHERE analysis_id=a.id AND workspace_id IS NOT a.workspace_id)+(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM batch_items WHERE analysis_id=a.id AND workspace_id IS NOT a.workspace_id)) AS foreignBindings,
+ h.revision AS reviewRevision,e.id AS reviewEventId,e.revision AS eventRevision,e.action,e.category AS reviewCategory,substr(e.reason,1,1000) AS reason,e.created_at AS reviewCreatedAt,CASE WHEN ${reviewSnapshotBytes}<=262144 THEN e.machine_snapshot_json ELSE NULL END AS reviewSnapshot,e.machine_sha256 AS reviewMachineHash,
+ (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM review_events WHERE analysis_id=a.id AND workspace_id=a.workspace_id) AS reviewEventCount
+ FROM analyses a JOIN workspaces w ON w.id=a.workspace_id JOIN workspace_memberships m ON m.workspace_id=w.id
+ LEFT JOIN assets ra ON ra.id=a.reference_asset_id AND ra.workspace_id=a.workspace_id
+ LEFT JOIN assets ca ON ca.id=a.candidate_asset_id AND ca.workspace_id=a.workspace_id
+ LEFT JOIN analysis_product_snapshots s ON s.analysis_id=a.id AND s.workspace_id=a.workspace_id
+ LEFT JOIN review_heads h ON h.analysis_id=a.id AND h.workspace_id=a.workspace_id
+ LEFT JOIN review_events e ON e.id=h.latest_event_id AND e.analysis_id=a.id AND e.workspace_id=a.workspace_id`;
+function matches(row:ReleaseRow,state:ReleaseFilters["state"]){return state==="all"||(state==="pending_review"&&row.eligibility==="needs_review")||(state==="blocked"&&row.eligibility==="blocked")||(state==="decided"&&["approve","reject"].includes(row.human.action??""));}
+export async function listReleases(db:D1Database,access:RequestAccessContext,filters:ReleaseFilters):Promise<ReleasePage> {
+  const owner=await requireReleaseOwner(db,access),evaluatedAt=new Date().toISOString();
+  const filterHash=await sha256(canonical({workspaceId:owner.workspaceId,sku:filters.sku,productId:filters.productId,batchId:filters.batchId,state:filters.state}));
+  let cursor:{createdAt:string;id:string}|null=null;
+  if(filters.cursor) {
+    try {if(!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(filters.cursor))throw new Error();const c=JSON.parse(atob(filters.cursor.replaceAll("-","+").replaceAll("_","/")));if(c.version!==1||c.filterHash!==filterHash||Object.keys(c).length!==4||typeof c.createdAt!=="string"||c.createdAt.length>64||!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(c.createdAt))||typeof c.id!=="string"||!safeId.test(c.id))throw new Error();cursor=c;}
+    catch {throw new ReleaseError("invalid_cursor","Cursor is invalid or belongs to another search.");}
+  }
+  const clauses=["a.workspace_id=?","w.status='active'","m.user_id=?","m.role='owner'"],values:unknown[]=[owner.workspaceId,owner.userId];
+  if(filters.sku){clauses.push("CASE WHEN json_valid(s.snapshot_json) THEN json_extract(s.snapshot_json,'$.profile.skuLabel') ELSE NULL END=?");values.push(filters.sku);}
+  if(filters.productId){clauses.push("s.product_id=?");values.push(filters.productId);}
+  if(filters.batchId){clauses.push("EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM batch_items bi JOIN batches b ON b.id=bi.batch_id AND b.workspace_id=bi.workspace_id WHERE bi.analysis_id=a.id AND bi.workspace_id=a.workspace_id AND bi.batch_id=?)");values.push(filters.batchId);}
+  if(cursor){clauses.push("(a.created_at<? OR (a.created_at=? AND a.id<?))");values.push(cursor.createdAt,cursor.createdAt,cursor.id);}
+  const result=await db.prepare(`${projection} WHERE ${clauses.join(" AND ")} ORDER BY a.created_at DESC,a.id DESC LIMIT ?`).bind(...values,RELEASE_SCAN_LIMIT+1).all<StoredReleaseRow>();
+  if(!result.success)throw new Error("Release snapshot read failed");
+  const rows:ReleaseRow[]=[];let consumed=0;
+  for(const source of result.results.slice(0,RELEASE_SCAN_LIMIT)){const row=await evaluateRelease(source,evaluatedAt);consumed++;if(matches(row,filters.state))rows.push(row);if(rows.length===filters.limit)break;}
+  const hasMore=result.results.length>consumed,last=result.results[consumed-1];
+  const nextCursor=hasMore&&last?btoa(JSON.stringify({version:1,filterHash,createdAt:last.createdAt,id:last.id})).replaceAll("+","-").replaceAll("/","_").replace(/=+$/u,""):null;
+  return {rows,policy:await releasePolicy(),evaluatedAt,pagination:{limit:filters.limit,scanLimit:RELEASE_SCAN_LIMIT,scanned:consumed,scanWindowExhausted:consumed===RELEASE_SCAN_LIMIT&&hasMore,hasMore,nextCursor,order:"created_at DESC, id DESC"}};
+}

+import type {ProductSnapshot} from "@/lib/products/types";
+export type Eligibility = "eligible" | "needs_review" | "blocked";
+export type ReleaseStateFilter = "all" | "pending_review" | "blocked" | "decided";
+export interface ReleaseFilters {sku:string;productId:string;batchId:string;state:ReleaseStateFilter;limit:number;cursor:string;}
+export interface ReleaseRow {
+  analysisId:string;createdAt:string;batchId:string|null;productSnapshot:ProductSnapshot|null;productSnapshotJson:string|null;productSnapshotHash:string|null;
+  machine:{status:string;verdict:string|null;qaEngineVersion:string|null;riskPolicyVersion:string|null;modelPolicyVersion:string|null;completedAt:string|null;issueCount:number;limitations:Array<{type:string;message:string}>;sourceHash:string|null;sourceComplete:boolean;sourceSnapshotJson:string};
+  assets:{referenceAvailable:boolean;candidateAvailable:boolean};
+  human:{action:string|null;category:string|null;reason:string|null;revision:number;createdAt:string|null;machineHash:string|null};
+  eligibility:Eligibility;reasons:Array<{code:string;message:string}>;evaluatedAt:string;policyVersion:string;policyHash:string;
+}
+export interface ReleasePage {
+  rows:ReleaseRow[];policy:{version:string;hash:string;description:string};evaluatedAt:string;
+  pagination:{limit:number;scanLimit:number;scanned:number;scanWindowExhausted:boolean;hasMore:boolean;nextCursor:string|null;order:string};
+}
+export class ReleaseError extends Error {
+  constructor(public code:string,message:string,public status=400){super(message);this.name="ReleaseError";}
+}
